# Skills · S · part 55

775 entries, seedance-copyright to segmentation-strategy. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **seedance-copyright**: Check prompts against Seedance 2.0 content policy, apply safe IP substitutions, and navigate real-person and brand restrictions. Updated with live Feb 2026 enforcement data from Disney, Paramount, MPA, and SAG-AFTRA actions. Use before every generation that involves named characters, franchise IP, celebrity likenesses, brand logos, or streaming originals. ([Kingdaddy007/my-os](https://github.com/Kingdaddy007/my-os/tree/HEAD/global/skills/seedance/skills/seedance-copyright) · ★5)
- **seedance-core**: Foundation for writing Seedance prompts when generating video via the Higgsfield platform. Always load this first — it carries the master template, tagging conventions, golden rules, decisiveness rule, dialogue/audio handling, and the intake checklist. Other seedance-* skills extend it with shot-type-specific rules. ([spindle79/show-sidekick-narrative-pack](https://github.com/spindle79/show-sidekick-narrative-pack/tree/HEAD/packs/seedance/skills/agents/seedance-core))

Use for course trailers, coaching ads, educational content promos, masterclass teasers, webinar promotions, or any educational product video. Triggers on course, coaching, masterclass, webinar, tutorial, education, teaching, training, class, program, academy. ([rediumvex/ai-video-generator-claude](https://github.com/rediumvex/ai-video-generator-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/04-course-promo) · ★492 · 3 copies)

- **seedance-debugger**: 诊断 Seedance 2.0 生成视频时出现的常见问题（人物 ID 漂移/双胞胎/字幕/Logo/风格漂移/延长跳变/画质劣化/特效不对/中文发音/音色不准/结尾噪音 等 12 类），定位根因并给出修复后的提示词。用于"我的提示词生成出来不对"、"视频里出现奇怪的字幕"、"人脸不像参考图"、"出现两个一样的人物"、"风格变了"、"怎么修这个提示词"等触发场景。 ([cclank/lanshu-awesome-ai-video-kit](https://github.com/cclank/lanshu-awesome-ai-video-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/seedance-debugger) · ★463 · 3 copies)
- **seedance-directing**: The fallback to omni-directing (Gemini Omni is the default video model). Use when writing a Seedance 2.5 video prompt (or any reference-driven video model) for a shot with people, a face, acting, dialogue, an action beat or several cuts in one generation; when clips feel "AI", lifeless, stiff, mugging, floaty, glossy or evenly paced; when a take is right except for one element; when the final lost what the draft had; or when building a scene from several angles. The PJ Ace / Chris Capel "Nexus" method, adapted to our routes. ([Trueflutter/film-studio](https://github.com/Trueflutter/film-studio/tree/HEAD/film-studio/skills/seedance-directing))

- **seedance-director**: Режиссёр Dreamina / Seedance 2.5 — вести диалог о создании и правке видео, а не просто исполнять. Использовать ВСЕГДА, когда пользователь хочет видео/ролик/рекламу через SeedFlow или Dreamina, присылает фото/видео товара, просит «сделай красиво», или спрашивает, что можно сделать с клипом. Скилл знает возможности Seedance 2.5, синтаксис промпта (роли референсов, скобки, таймлайн), кредиты и инструменты dream_status / dream_generate / dream_wait / dream_edit / dream_cancel. - **seedance-director**: Seedance 2.0 新媒体视频总监。从需求拆解到素材编排到可直接复制的提示词，全链路引导式输出。覆盖电商广告、短剧、科普、MV、Vlog、视频编辑等全场景。当用户提到 Seedance、即梦、想拍视频、写视频提示词时触发。 ([JOHNadonis/claude-code-skills](https://github.com/JOHNadonis/claude-code-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/seedance-director))
